#### Article 39b. Exceptional temporary support to farmers and SMEs particularly affected by the COVID-19 crisis[^M_E_8f1657zh-dtcq-4le7-100u-665744b7730ae][^V_E_dc2812ba-998d-67dd-1468-77cd3d6a7004]

*Extent: E*

1. Support under this measure shall provide emergency assistance to farmers and SMEs particularly affected by the COVID-19 crisis, aiming at ensuring continuity of their business activity, subject to the conditions set out in this Article.

2. Support shall be granted to farmers, as well as to SMEs active in processing, marketing or development of agricultural products covered by Annex I to the TFEU or cotton, except fishery products. The output of the production process may be a product not covered by that Annex.

3. The relevant authority[^V_E_f878c331-f85f-8203-7529-d8a41e5480fc] shall target the support to beneficiaries who are most affected by the COVID-19 crisis, by determining, on the basis of available evidence, eligibility conditions and, where considered appropriate by the relevant authority[^V_E_067a2f19-cfa5-29f8-4bff-3215d5a01f15] concerned, selection criteria, which shall be objective and non-discriminatory.

4.[^V_E_b262cd5d-2e1a-d525-25d7-f53a882b8753] The support shall take the form of a lump sum payment to be paid by 31 December 2021, based on applications for support approved by the competent authority by 30 June 2021. [^V_E_af87b570-65ee-ca55-5b70-86e729cf20e4]... The level of payment may be differentiated by categories of beneficiaries, in accordance with objective and non-discriminatory criteria.

5. The maximum amount of support shall not exceed EUR 7 000 per farmer and EUR 50 000 per SME.

6. When granting support under this Article, the relevant authority[^V_E_3288ea0d-9642-fb47-784f-bd04701deaf7] shall take into account the support granted under Regulation 508/2014[^V_E_cf64b1e0-8824-5139-f4be-f1adaee35e28] [^c-0feec2d5fff313e0f608ff3157d63b5f]... or private schemes to respond to the impact of the COVID-19 crisis.

#### Article 39b. Exceptional temporary support to farmers and SMEs particularly affected by the COVID-19 crisis[^M_E_1poj1njn-kre9-fbog-kqr7-665744b773345][^V_S_64ee8946-4696-2466-7fba-4cd00dc93e94]

*Extent: S*

1. Support under this measure shall provide emergency assistance to farmers and SMEs particularly affected by the COVID-19 crisis, aiming at ensuring continuity of their business activity, subject to the conditions set out in this Article.

2. Support shall be granted to farmers, as well as to SMEs active in processing, marketing or development of agricultural products covered by Annex I to the TFEU or cotton, except fishery products. The output of the production process may be a product not covered by that Annex.

3. The relevant authority[^V_S_03a9ef62-c29b-3afb-4838-53a625017005] shall target the support to beneficiaries who are most affected by the COVID-19 crisis, by determining, on the basis of available evidence, eligibility conditions and, where considered appropriate by the relevant authority[^V_S_ada46843-67b8-812c-5161-b8c2e76cb2e6] concerned, selection criteria, which shall be objective and non-discriminatory.

4.[^V_S_56d83860-f3f5-ca14-00fe-d2fa1ad1e364] The support shall take the form of a lump sum payment to be paid by 31 December 2021, based on applications for support approved by the competent authority by 30 June 2021. [^V_S_7564bc41-a0c3-b378-c803-2bbb25c27876]... The level of payment may be differentiated by categories of beneficiaries, in accordance with objective and non-discriminatory criteria.

5. The maximum amount of support shall not exceed GBP 5,833[^V_S_befe568b-69da-140b-611e-7a3c5c58266e] per farmer and GBP 41,667[^V_S_1a6cfc8f-982d-6858-8783-cd8910a931a2] per SME.

6. When granting support under this Article, the relevant authority[^V_S_58ecd784-1509-9b53-1211-84e1d212f360] shall take into account the support granted under Regulation 508/2014 or direct payment support[^V_S_60960b53-a43a-dea0-7d7c-b824c21b28ab] or private schemes to respond to the impact of the COVID-19 crisis.

#### Article 40. Financing of complementary national direct payments for Croatia[^c-f2899f674c188a820fce6719636d4521]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 41. Rules on the implementation of the measures

The the appropriate authority may make regulations[^c-e96db7c24a4ff216175bb7636bd12f77], laying down rules on the implementation of the measures in this section concerning:

- ((a)) procedures for selection of authorities or bodies offering farm and forestry advisory services, farm management or farm relief services and the degressivity of the aid under the advisory services measure referred to in Article 15;
- ((b)) the assessment by the Managing Authority[^c-a7e0cd8147da705fc75a3453aac23c53] of the progress of the business plan, payment options as well as modalities for access to other measures for young farmers under the farm and business development measure referred to in Article 19;
- ((c)) conversion to units other than those used in Annex II, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5177a2fcfb97442748924dfa2933e78a] and conversion rates of animals to livestock units (LU) under the measures referred to in Articles 28, 29, 33 and 34;
- ((d)) the possibility of using standard assumptions of additional costs and income foregone under the measures of Articles 28 to 31, 33 and 34 and criteria for its calculation;
- ((e)) calculation of the amount of support where an operation is eligible for support under more than one measures.

[^c-e34ec9a6c3739fbc8d8be5a0a5219aa9]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### LEADER

##### Article 42. LEADER local action groups

1.[^M_F_be070d57-9248-4259-bef7-37ef7666dac2] In addition to the tasks referred to in Article 34 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_57cadb6a-2aaf-4fad-84a1-30dcd473fa94] [^c-3db49ecad9fd463caaf5a5e5ea6bb168]... local action groups may also perform additional tasks delegated to them by the Managing Authority and/or the paying agency. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_57cadb6a-2aaf-4fad-84a1-30dcd473fa94]In addition to the tasks referred to in Article 34 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 local action groups may also perform additional tasks delegated to them by the Managing Authority and/or the paying agency. *(Extent: W)*

2. Local action groups may request the payment of an advance from the competent paying agency if such possibility is provided for in the rural development programme. The amount of the advances shall not exceed 50 % of the public support related to the running and animation costs.

##### Article 43 (affected by repeal, see note). LEADER start-up kit[^c-469eb9393e40a3a7b571b2d92d0c51f8]

Support for LEADER local development may also include a "LEADER start-up kit" for local communities who did not implement LEADER in the 2007-2013 programming period. The "LEADER start-up kit" shall consist of support for capacity building and small pilot projects. Support under the "LEADER start-up kit" shall not be conditional on the submission of a LEADER local development strategy. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-469eb9393e40a3a7b571b2d92d0c51f8]

##### Article 44. LEADER co-operation activities

1. The support referred to in point (c) of Article 35(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be granted to

- (a)[^c-71ace7553cfd3e50e83080cdeebe8840] co-operation projects within or between constituent nations (inter-territorial co-operation) or co-operation projects with territories in third countries (transnational co-operation);
- (b) preparatory technical support for inter-territorial and transnational co-operation projects, on condition that local action groups are able to demonstrate that they are envisaging the implementation of a concrete project.

2. Apart from other local action groups, the partners of a local action group funded by support for rural development[^c-afdad81e203d03ce4d606636d837d314] may be:

- (a) a group of local public and private partners in a rural territory that is implementing a local development strategy [^c-07e9b0908c255bdfd1c9570978ab9d22]...;
- (b) a group of local public and private partners in a non- rural territory that is implementing a local development strategy.

3. In cases where co-operation projects are not selected by the local action groups, the relevant authority[^c-0fdae7c874fb9fd7f11b6b359e64f641] shall establish a system of ongoing application.

They shall make public the national or regional administrative procedures concerning the selection of transnational co-operation projects and a list of eligible costs at the latest two years after the date of approval of their rural development programmes.

Approval of co-operation projects by the competent authority shall take place no later than four months after the date of submission of the project application.

4.[^c-c3bfdbd3925d2c11918e22d853deafd3] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### CHAPTER II — Common provisions for several measures

#### Article 45. Investments[^M_E_l5z7glmf-30jb-9nk5-ounq-66560341312bd]

*Extent: E+S+N.I.*

1. In order to be eligible for support for rural development[^c-64488261d4de97cc5b8ca4866101e906], investment operations shall be preceded by an assessment of the expected environmental impact in accordance with law specific to that kind of investment where the investment is likely to have negative effects on the environment.

2. Expenditure that is eligible for support for rural development shall be limited to:

- (a) the construction, acquisition, including leasing, or improvement of immovable property;
- (b) the purchase or lease purchase of new machinery and equipment up to the market value of the asset;
- (c) general costs linked to expenditure referred to in points (a) and (b), such as architect, engineer and consultation fees, fees relating to advice on environmental and economic sustainability, including feasibility studies. Feasibility studies shall remain eligible expenditure even where, based on their results, no expenditure under points (a) and (b) is made;
- (d) the following intangible investments: acquisition or development of computer software and acquisitions of patents, licenses, copyrights, trademarks;
- (e) the costs of establishing forest management plans and their equivalent.

3. In the case of agricultural investments, the purchase of agricultural production rights, payment entitlements, animals, annual plants and their planting shall not be eligible for investment support. However, in case of the restoration of agricultural production potential damaged by natural disasters or catastrophic events, in accordance with point (b) of Article 18(1), expenditure for the purchase of animals may be eligible expenditure.

4. Beneficiaries of investment related support may request the payment of an advance of up to 50 % of the public aid related to the investment from the competent paying agencies if that option is included in the rural development programme.

5. Where support is provided through a financial instrument established in accordance with Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, working capital may be eligible expenditure. Such eligible expenditure shall not exceed EUR 200 000 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-dd4f030aba1fecc61b84ae959750a678]GBP 166,667 or 30 % of the total amount of the eligible expenditure for the investment, whichever is the higher.

6. In order to take account of the special characteristics of particular types of investments, the appropriate authority may make regulations[^c-da2518b251838ea8926c9b91bf5df405], laying down the conditions under which other costs connected with leasing contracts, second hand equipment may be considered to be eligible expenditure and specifying the types of renewable energy infrastructure that are to be eligible for support.

7. Paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 shall not apply where support is provided in the form of financial instruments.

#### Article 45. Investments[^M_E_kzf2uk1f-ywps-l7il-gb2k-665603413123a]

*Extent: W*

1. In order to be eligible for support for rural development[^V_W_53b4672e-0f30-0050-87e4-887b6717e6aa], investment operations shall be preceded by an assessment of the expected environmental impact in accordance with law specific to that kind of investment where the investment is likely to have negative effects on the environment.

2. Expenditure that is eligible for support for rural development shall be limited to:

- (a) the construction, acquisition, including leasing, or improvement of immovable property;
- (b) the purchase or lease purchase of new machinery and equipment up to the market value of the asset;
- (c) general costs linked to expenditure referred to in points (a) and (b), such as architect, engineer and consultation fees, fees relating to advice on environmental and economic sustainability, including feasibility studies. Feasibility studies shall remain eligible expenditure even where, based on their results, no expenditure under points (a) and (b) is made;
- (d) the following intangible investments: acquisition or development of computer software and acquisitions of patents, licenses, copyrights, trademarks;
- (e) the costs of establishing forest management plans and their equivalent.

3. In the case of agricultural investments, the purchase of agricultural production rights, payment entitlements, animals, annual plants and their planting shall not be eligible for investment support. However, in case of the restoration of agricultural production potential damaged by natural disasters or catastrophic events, in accordance with point (b) of Article 18(1), expenditure for the purchase of animals may be eligible expenditure.

4. Beneficiaries of investment related support may request the payment of an advance of up to 50 % of the public aid related to the investment from the competent paying agencies if that option is included in the rural development programme.

5.[^V_W_90a7d75b-a8ef-be70-c715-5c0ca73116f9] Where support is provided through a financial instrument established in accordance with Article 37 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, working capital may be eligible expenditure. Such eligible expenditure shall not exceed £200 000[^c-afe2d7b683bea133bbceb442e796e70f] or 30 % of the total amount of the eligible expenditure for the investment, whichever is the higher.

6. In order to take account of the special characteristics of particular types of investments, the appropriate authority may make regulations[^V_W_a433d831-8aee-efa4-29c5-e55c11f40920], laying down the conditions under which other costs connected with leasing contracts, second hand equipment may be considered to be eligible expenditure and specifying the types of renewable energy infrastructure that are to be eligible for support.

7.[^V_W_8976a120-85d7-cbbc-e754-de6792b414a6] Paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 shall not apply where support is provided in the form of financial instruments.

#### Article 46. Investments in irrigation

1. Without prejudice to Article 45 of this Regulation, in the case of irrigation in new and existing irrigated areas, only investments that fulfil the conditions in this Article shall be considered as eligible expenditure.

2.[^c-d03b24afdda021c9ec29ecb2d5fa5c6a] A river basin management plan, as required under the terms of the Water Framework Directive and as defined by legislation implementing the Water Framework Directive in each of the constituent nations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-869316dcdef7e2a6f7422c82b8ff4da4], shall have been notified to the appropriate authority, or, before IP completion day, to the Commission, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-86102d39265b278338e902ec5d693f4c] for the entire area in which the investment is to take place, as well as in any other areas whose environment may be affected by the investment. The requirements which apply under the river basin management plan by virtue of Article 11 of the Water Framework Directive and of relevance to the agricultural sector shall have been specified.

3. Water metering enabling measurement of water use at the level of the supported investment shall be in place or shall be put in place as part of the investment.

4. An investment in an improvement to an existing irrigation installation or element of irrigation infrastructure shall be eligible only if it is assessed ex ante as offering potential water savings of a minimum of between 5 % and 25 % according to the technical parameters of the existing installation or infrastructure.

If the investment affects bodies of ground- or surface water whose status has been identified as less than good in the relevant river basin management plan for reasons related to water quantity:

- (a) the investment shall ensure an effective reduction in water use, at the level of the investment, amounting to at least 50 % of the potential water saving made possible by the investment;
- (b) in the case of an investment on a single agricultural holding, it shall also result in a reduction to the holding's total water use amounting to at least 50 % of the potential water saving made possible at the level of the investment. The total water use of the holding shall include water sold by the holding.

None of the conditions in paragraph 4 shall apply to an investment in an existing installation which affects only energy efficiency or to an investment in the creation of a reservoir or to an investment in the use of recycled water which does not affect a body of ground or surface water.

5. An investment resulting in a net increase of the irrigated area affecting a given body of ground or surface water shall be eligible only if:

- (a) the status of the water body has not been identified as less than good in the relevant river basin management plan for reasons related to water quantity; and
- (b) an environmental analysis shows that there will be no significant negative environmental impact from the investment; such an environmental impact analysis shall be either carried out by or approved by the competent authority and may also refer to groups of holdings.

Areas which are not irrigated but in which an irrigation installation was active in the recent past, to be established and justified in the programme, may be considered as irrigated areas for the purpose of determining the net increase of the irrigated area.

6. By way of derogation from point (a) of paragraph 5 investments resulting in a net increase of the irrigated area may still be eligible if:

- (a) the investment is combined with an investment in an existing irrigation installation or element of irrigation infrastructure assessed ex ante as offering potential water savings of a minimum of between 5 % and 25 % according to the technical parameters of the existing installation or infrastructure and
- (b) the investment ensures an effective reduction in water use, at the level of the investment as a whole, amounting to at least 50 % of the potential water saving made possible by the investment in the existing irrigation installation or element of infrastructure.

Furthermore, by way of derogation, the condition in point (a) of paragraph 5 shall not apply to investments in the establishment of a new irrigation installation supplied with water from an existing reservoir approved by the competent authorities before 31 October 2013 if the following conditions are met:

- the reservoir in question is identified in the relevant river basin management plan and is subject to the control requirements set out in article 11(3)(e) of the Water Framework Directive;
- on 31 October 2013, there was in force either a maximum limit on total abstractions from the reservoir or a minimum required level of flow in water bodies affected by the reservoir;
- that maximum limit or minimum required level of flow complies with the conditions set out in Article 4 of the Water Framework Directive; and
- the investment in question does not result in abstractions beyond the maximum limit in force on 31 October 2013 or result in a reduction of the level of flow in affected water bodies below the minimum required level in force on 31 October 2013.

#### Article 47. Rules for area related payments

1. The number of hectares to which a commitment pursuant to Articles 28, 29 and 34 applies may vary from year to year where:

- (a) this possibility is provided for in the rural development programme;
- (b) the commitment in question does not apply to fixed parcels; and
- (c) the achievement of the commitment's objective is not jeopardised.

2. Where all or part of the land under commitment or the entire holding is transferred to another person during the period of that commitment, the commitment, or part thereof corresponding to the land transferred, may be taken over for the remainder of the period by that other person or may expire and reimbursement shall not be required in respect of the period during which the commitment was effective.

3. Where a beneficiary is unable to continue to comply with commitments given because the holding or part of the holding is re-parcelled or is the subject of public land consolidation measures or land consolidation measures approved by the competent public authorities, the relevant authority[^c-e165d613775bb134298bc46ae170e4ac] shall take the measures necessary to allow the commitments to be adapted to the new situation of the holding. If such adaptation proves impossible, the commitment shall expire and reimbursement shall not be required in respect of the period during which the commitment was effective.

4. Reimbursement of the aid received shall not be required in cases of force majeure and exceptional circumstances as referred to in Article 2 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013.

5. Paragraph 2, as regards cases of transfer of the entire holding, and paragraph 4 shall also apply to commitments under Article 33.

6. In order to ensure the efficient implementation of area related measures and proper management of public money[^c-c272b0e277d3fbd33cbdf3c978d0e316], the *(Extent: W)*[^c-0076787f3ec3b35e161e2b269fbf96bd] the appropriate authority may make regulations[^c-b409d040778939cfe1e0336a806a8e2e] laying down conditions applicable to conversion or adjustment of commitments under the measures referred to in Articles 28, 29, 33 and 34 and, specifying other situations in which reimbursement of the aid shall not be required.

#### Article 48. Revision clause

A revision clause shall be provided for operations undertaken pursuant to Articles 28, 29, 33 and 34 in order to ensure their adjustment in the case of amendments to the relevant mandatory standards, requirements or obligations referred to in those Articles beyond which the commitments have to go. The revision clause shall also cover adjustments needed to avoid double funding of the practices referred to in Article 43 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 in the case of amendments to those practices. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-6701a1797e7369e4161bedb055dac4df]

Operations undertaken pursuant to Articles 28, 29, 33 and 34 which extend beyond the current programming period shall contain a revision clause in order to allow for their adjustment to the legal framework *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d9c561acede2fcbeefcf437feb48b41e] in force after the current programming period[^c-4ef36c8954e71315b0252f50218a790d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d9c561acede2fcbeefcf437feb48b41e].

If such adjustment is not accepted by the beneficiary, the commitment shall expire and reimbursement shall not be required in respect of the period during which the commitment was effective.

#### Article 49. Selection of operations[^M_E_i76hwiru-nsxs-l26d-1jwj-665608c84a58c]

*Extent: E+S+N.I.*

1. Without prejudice to point (d) of Article 34(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the Managing Authority of the rural development programme shall define selection criteria for operations following consultation with the Monitoring Committee. Selection criteria shall aim to ensure equal treatment of applicants, better use of financial resources and targeting of measures in accordance with the [^c-983a1cc3f7682ed27fe007fe53af60ef]... priorities for rural development. In defining and applying selection criteria the principle of proportionality shall be taken into account in relation to the size of the operation.

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, in exceptional and duly justified cases where it is not possible to establish selection criteria due to the nature of the type of operations concerned, the Managing Authority may define another selection method to be described in the rural development programme following consultation with the Monitoring Committee.

2.[^c000008] The relevant authority[^c-4c216bc898e2133826be7e99892593ae] responsible for the selection of operations shall ensure that operations, with the exception of operations under point (b) of Article 18(1), point (d) of Article 24(1) and Articles 28 to 31, 33, 34 and 36 to 39b, are selected in accordance with the selection criteria referred to in paragraph 1 of this Article and according to a transparent and well-documented procedure.

3. Beneficiaries may be selected on the basis of calls for proposals, applying economic, social and environmental efficiency criteria.

4. Paragraphs 1 and 2 shall not apply where support is provided in the form of financial instruments.

*Extent: W*

1. Without prejudice to point (d) of Article 34(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the Managing Authority of the rural development programme shall define selection criteria for operations [^c-f3a30addf3119c8a3fc8d2168159653f].... Selection criteria shall aim to ensure equal treatment of applicants, better use of financial resources and targeting of measures in accordance with the [^V_W_a2ae0105-e576-a472-10df-c4cbc50d48d1]... priorities for rural development. In defining and applying selection criteria the principle of proportionality shall be taken into account in relation to the size of the operation.

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, in exceptional and duly justified cases where it is not possible to establish selection criteria due to the nature of the type of operations concerned, the Managing Authority may define another selection method to be described in the rural development programme[^V_W_94d5fe93-c4ed-0bff-dd3c-7ed10d18e04f] [^c-f3a30addf3119c8a3fc8d2168159653f]....

2.[^V_W_85044bd7-b316-c4a0-da07-ffc2312e9840][^V_W_6681a2bd-335e-b0f7-a2ac-df97bf60544e] The relevant authority[^V_W_efd2e77f-6b3b-5ad9-0a95-693d6062f87d] responsible for the selection of operations shall ensure that operations, with the exception of operations under point (b) of Article 18(1), point (d) of Article 24(1) and Articles 28 to 31, 33, 34 and 36 to 39a[^c-1ae57c1c6c79f6fa07178cc6fd2c42d3], are selected in accordance with the selection criteria referred to in paragraph 1 of this Article and according to a transparent and well-documented procedure.

3. Beneficiaries may be selected on the basis of calls for proposals, applying economic, social and environmental efficiency criteria.

4. Paragraphs 1 and 2 shall not apply where support is provided in the form of financial instruments.

#### Article 50. Rural area definition

For the purposes of this Regulation the Managing Authority shall define "rural area" at programme level. The relevant authority[^c-a104ba04eb5dca24449074b3c0f60960] may establish such a definition for a measure or type of operation if duly justified.

### CHAPTER III — Technical assistance and networking

#### Article 51 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Funding technical assistance[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-61c0210edb482f9bcde12e552989cf3f][^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] At the initiative of the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] relevant authority[^c-000f6f80937fbc4785922bffbd05292b] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] up to 4 % of the total amount of each rural development programme may be devoted to the tasks referred to in Article 59 of Regulation (EC) No 1303/2013 and costs related to preparatory work for the delimitation of areas facing natural or other specific constraints referred to in Article 32. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

Costs relating to the certification body referred to in Article 9 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 shall not be eligible under this paragraph. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

Within that 4 % limit an amount shall be reserved for establishing and operating the national rural network referred to in Article 54. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, Member States for which the total amount of Union support for rural development for the years 2014-2020 as laid down in Annex I to this Regulation is less than EUR 1 800 million may, after the extension of their programmes in accordance with Article 1 of Regulation (EU) 2020/2220, decide to devote 5 % of the total amount of each rural development programme to tasks referred to in Article 59 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013.[^M_F_2df1316a-c6c9-4010-c74a-d927b45ce184] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-55b045bbbd11cb76db597fec45737c3b] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

[^c-199a0216487585c38dd9a18e18805e3e]... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] Networking by the national rural network shall aim to: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] increase the involvement of stakeholders in the implementation of rural development; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (c (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] inform the broader public and potential beneficiaries on rural development policy and funding opportunities; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] Support for rural development[^c-754d3fc4c1dc62c7dd66a88a1f216b9d] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] under Article 51(2) shall be used: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] for the structures needed to run the network; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] for the preparation and implementation of an action plan covering at least the following: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

  - ((iii)) activities regarding the provision of training and networking for local action groups and in particular technical assistance for inter-territorial and transnational co-operation, facilitation of co-operation among local action groups and the search of partners for the measure referred to in Article 35; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

  - ((vi)) a communication plan including publicity and information concerning the rural development programme in agreement with the Managing Authorities and information and communication activities aimed at a broader public; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
  - ((vii)) [^c-178ce69e89a36e8d1f152b0ab1d8286e]... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

## TITLE IV — EIP FOR AGRICULTURAL PRODUCTIVITY AND SUSTAINABILITY

### Article 55 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Aims[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] The EIP for agricultural productivity and sustainability shall: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] promote a resource efficient, economically viable, productive, competitive, low emission, climate friendly and resilient agricultural and forestry sector, working towards agro-ecological production systems and working in harmony with the essential natural resources on which farming and forestry depend; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] help deliver a steady and sustainable supply of food, feed and biomaterials, including existing and new types; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (c (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] improve processes to preserve the environment, adapt to climate change and mitigate it; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (d (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] build bridges between cutting-edge research knowledge and technology and farmers, forest managers, rural communities, businesses, NGOs and advisory services. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] The EIP for agricultural productivity and sustainability shall seek to achieve its aims by: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] creating added value by better linking research and farming practice and encouraging the wider use of available innovation measures; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] promoting the faster and wider transposition of innovative solutions into practice; and *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (c (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] informing the scientific community about the research needs of farming practice. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] Support for rural development[^c-56bd0f91e8db191f201310f43ac5a8b5] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] shall contribute to the aims of the EIP for agricultural productivity and sustainability through support, in accordance with Article 35, of the EIP operational groups referred to in Article 56 *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] [^c-76d895f04de3cf23098f882119403122].... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

### Article 56 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Operational groups[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] EIP operational groups shall form part of the EIP for agricultural productivity and sustainability. They shall be set up by interested actors such as farmers, researchers, advisors and businesses involved in the agriculture and food sector, who are relevant for achieving the objectives of the EIP. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] EIP operational groups shall establish internal procedures that ensure, that their operation and decision-making is transparent and that situations of conflict of interest are avoided. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] The *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] relevant authority[^c-fe1c1b4b9b61b42c4a71ab696744a161] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] shall decide within the framework of their programmes to what extent they will support the operational groups. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

### Article 57 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Tasks of operational groups[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] EIP operational groups shall draw up a plan that contains the following: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] a description of the innovative project to be developed, tested, adapted or implemented; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] a description of the expected results and the contribution to the EIP objective of enhancing productivity and sustainable resource management. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] When implementing their innovative projects operational groups shall: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] make decisions on the elaboration and implementation of innovative actions; and *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]
- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] implement innovative actions through measures financed through the rural development programmes. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] Operational groups shall disseminate the results of their project *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2] [^c-53f44614360847e6d49477bc64c7133e].... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ce6c7e053c95cfc9ccc23db1ffe7c9c2]

### Article 58. Resources and their distribution[^M_E_dssk4wd6-2rdd-77kd-5ipz-66574624a3d14][^M_F_facd2f0d-3c8a-4ca3-fb73-f252b1f1395b]

*Extent: S*

1. Without prejudice to paragraphs 3, 5 and 7 of this Article, the core contribution to rural development under this Regulation (“core contribution”) is the amount determined by the Scottish Ministers.

2. The Scottish Ministers must determine the core contribution before the start of the relevant financial year.

3. The Scottish Ministers must include in the core contribution any funds transferred, or to be transferred, in respect of the relevant year under Articles 11(4A) and 14 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013.

4. When the core contribution has been determined, the Scottish Ministers must publish that amount as soon as practicable after that determination has been made.

5. The Scottish Ministers may review the core contribution determined under paragraph 2, and for that purpose may have regard to:

- (a) any transfer of funds under Articles 11(4A) and 14 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013;
- (b) any legislative change after the determination; and
- (c) such other matters as they consider appropriate.

6. A review under paragraph 5 (including a further review) may be carried out before or during the relevant financial year.

7. The Scottish Ministers may adjust the core contribution after carrying out a review under paragraph 5 provided that they do so before the end of the relevant financial year.

8. When the Scottish Ministers adjust the core contribution under paragraph 7 they must publish the adjusted amount as soon as practicable after making that adjustment.

9. In this Article, “financial year” means a period of 12 months beginning with 1 April.

### Article 58. Resources and their distribution[^M_E_vjwybker-d05u-ec39-wzma-66574624a3f79]

*Extent: E*

1.[^V_E_a21272dc-a736-6e91-0454-1de9c36a512e] Without prejudice to paragraphs 5, 6 and 7 of this Article, the core contribution to rural development under this Regulation (‘the core contribution’) for the period from 1 January 2014 to 31 December 2020 shall be EUR 5,195,417,491, in 2011 prices.

6.[^c-bb434c780e6b08eb7cac0f3a27087c41] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

7A.[^V_E_487bbcf2-f08f-a025-38df-476f660f8132] The Secretary of State may not make regulations under paragraph 7 without the consent of each of the relevant authorities for Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland.

Where the relevant authority for Wales, Scotland or Northern Ireland requests that the Secretary of State makes regulations under paragraph 7, the Secretary of State must have regard to that request.

### Article 59. Fund contribution[^M_E_o26fss0g-4o8c-t94v-6r8j-665755d16bc97]

*Extent: E*

1. The decision approving a rural development programme shall set the core contribution[^c-05134807431bcb8f1c0f242dca691d1c] to the programme. The decision shall clearly identify, where necessary, the appropriations allocated to the less developed regions.

2. The core[^c-acc3bc9fe521d0568b184594f2054db3] contribution shall be calculated on the basis of the amount of eligible public expenditure.

3.[^c-14667dda2ae19821c20ce243cbbbb358] The rural development programmes shall establish a single core contribution rate applicable to all measures. Where applicable, a separate core contribution rate shall be established for less-developed regions, as well as for transition regions. The maximum core contribution rate shall be:

- (a) 85 % of the eligible public expenditure in the less developed regions [^c-48aa64805164d0d503cbcaaf952e3d5d]...;
- (b) 75 % of the eligible public expenditure for all regions whose GDP per capita for the 2007-2013 period was less than 75 % of the average of the EU-25 for the reference period but whose GDP per capita is above 75 % of the GDP average of the EU-27;
- (c) 63 % of the eligible public expenditure for the transition regions other than those referred to in point (b) of this paragraph;
- (d) 53 % of the eligible public expenditure in the other regions.

The minimum core[^c-35c20616868bdd12195d74780d156b26] contribution rate shall be 20 %.

4. By way of derogation from paragraph 3, the maximum core[^c-9c419b58c6cb8d48c89e2aa5bb121544] contribution shall be:

- (a) 80 % for the measures referred to in Articles 14, 27 and 35, for the LEADER local development referred to in Article 32 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and for operations under point (a)(i) of Article 19(1). That rate may be increased to a maximum of 90 % for the programmes of less developed regions [^c-c16323ebdccc592e02c7235d8b87bffb]... and of transition regions referred to in points (b) and (c) of paragraph 3;
- (b) 75 % for operations contributing to the objectives of environment and climate change mitigation and adaptation under Article 17, points (a) and (b) of Article 21(1), Articles 28 29, 30 31 and 34;
- (c)[^c-d0fbea65f4339f11c604977bc1710dd4] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (d) the contribution rate applicable to the measure concerned increased by an additional 10 percentage points for contributions to financial instruments referred to in point (b) of Article 38(1) of Regulation 1303/2013;
- (e)[^c-3d054d4405cdf21b703fcc2da9303dbb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (ea)[^M_F_fcc5f55c-bf6d-4568-fba9-e14023938e7c] 100 % for operations receiving funding from additional resources referred to in Article 58a(1). Member States may establish a single, specific EAFRD contribution rate applicable to all those operations;
- (f)[^c-2a67918994232efe7af1d6b21a8cb9a5] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (g)[^c-2a67918994232efe7af1d6b21a8cb9a5] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (h)[^c-2a67918994232efe7af1d6b21a8cb9a5] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

5 (Extent: E+NI).[^c-448a8eb3c0851e13f271a5bc8397cbf1] At least 5% of the total core contribution to the rural development programme shall be reserved for LEADER. *(Extent: E+NI)*

6. At least 30 % of the total core[^c-374a82a1678c747170a59e094f4ef787] contribution to the rural development programme shall be reserved for measures under the following Articles: Article 17 for environment and climate related investments; Articles 21, 28, 29 and 30, with the exception of Water Framework Directive related payments; and Articles 31, 32 and 34.

[^c-e6d12f598aaa3789cb7b054d58267169]...

6a.[^M_F_79280fd7-5386-4885-bec8-dc9c2ee7a564] The [^c-acd77c10af61c17f90a596d370406f14]... support provided under Article 39b shall not exceed 2 % of the total core[^c-4e869ee45564332fcd77447f72d0dff1] contribution to the rural development programme [^c-b368b20890384089177a02759d9754b3]....

7.[^c-d208267a77a03c9fc58588fa0a313f04] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

8.[^c-b92dd7f89f9cfffbacd9954e4ce104e8] An expenditure receiving support for rural development shall not be co-financed by way of a contribution from under Regulation 508/2014 or CMO support [^c-bcb5b699a680f8e47d4cb816bad838be]....

9. Public expenditure on aid to enterprises shall comply with the aid limits laid down in respect of State aid, unless this Regulation provides otherwise.

- (e) 100 % for operations receiving funding from funds transferred to the core contribution[^V_S_a5382e48-6099-3adb-3c92-efe28caad8e7] in application of Article 11(4A) and Article 14[^c-c8bf9a4bd57f1b7f8a2ba3d33ff9990c] of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013;
- (ea) 100 % for operations receiving funding from additional resources referred to in Article 58a(1). Member States may establish a single, specific EAFRD contribution rate applicable to all those operations;
- (f)[^V_S_e2f032f3-3e6f-bc24-6203-29196659c0aa] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### Article 59. Fund contribution[^M_E_ft78ik6e-tsk0-cxwz-576w-665755d16bc27]

*Extent: N.I.*

1. The decision approving a rural development programme shall set the core contribution[^V_NI_e212c050-3b46-0c20-0649-72d8c2afb509] to the programme. The decision shall clearly identify, where necessary, the appropriations allocated to the less developed regions.

2. The core[^V_NI_c60b89ac-5a6f-0d2d-eb76-0f1e4a4405f6] contribution shall be calculated on the basis of the amount of eligible public expenditure.

3.[^V_NI_8be3186f-b83a-95dd-fb67-bb98822f7bd1] The rural development programmes shall establish a single core contribution rate applicable to all measures. Where applicable, a separate core contribution rate shall be established for less-developed regions, as well as for transition regions. The maximum core contribution rate shall be:

- (a)[^V_NI_60b21a50-a429-2e88-087d-da92be3061ea] 85 % of the eligible public expenditure in the less developed regions [^V_NI_ccc41df4-55fd-c760-d264-d17b2db34942]...;
- (b) 75 % of the eligible public expenditure for all regions whose GDP per capita for the 2007-2013 period was less than 75 % of the average of the EU-25 for the reference period but whose GDP per capita is above 75 % of the GDP average of the EU-27;
- (c) 63 % of the eligible public expenditure for the transition regions other than those referred to in point (b) of this paragraph;
- (d) 53 % of the eligible public expenditure in the other regions.

The minimum core[^V_NI_1b83facd-07da-9d4f-587e-7bb0c98a2061] contribution rate shall be 20 %.

4. By way of derogation from paragraph 3, the maximum core[^V_NI_03643147-15b9-70be-cc18-c17fc2fa3d57] contribution shall be:

- (a) 80 % for the measures referred to in Articles 14, 27 and 35, for the LEADER local development referred to in Article 32 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and for operations under point (a)(i) of Article 19(1). That rate may be increased to a maximum of 90 % for the programmes of less developed regions [^V_NI_fa4c820a-bb4d-d198-9ebb-1c27f82a94c3]... and of transition regions referred to in points (b) and (c) of paragraph 3;
- (b) 75 % for operations contributing to the objectives of environment and climate change mitigation and adaptation under Article 17, points (a) and (b) of Article 21(1), Articles 28 29, 30 31 and 34;
- (c)[^V_NI_ee6143ce-778a-2d08-41ce-ba858c0501ee] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (d) the contribution rate applicable to the measure concerned increased by an additional 10 percentage points for contributions to financial instruments referred to in point (b) of Article 38(1) of Regulation 1303/2013;
- (e) 100 % for operations receiving funding from funds transferred to the core contribution[^V_NI_a6f7f0b8-dd99-afc6-8f0e-5c242f039994] in application of Article 136a(1) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 and Article 7(2) and Article 14(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013;
- (ea)[^V_NI_b53e7796-4cc4-93a0-f898-e5d81b436f53] 100 % for operations receiving funding from additional resources referred to in Article 58a(1). Member States may establish a single, specific EAFRD contribution rate applicable to all those operations;
- (f)[^V_NI_99076b44-7a9f-f81d-0596-fb4d34409b8b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (g)[^V_NI_99076b44-7a9f-f81d-0596-fb4d34409b8b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (h)[^V_NI_99076b44-7a9f-f81d-0596-fb4d34409b8b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

5 (Extent: E+NI).[^V_NI_82ab9b25-faef-e23c-b171-9d568e6e6faf] At least 5% of the total core contribution to the rural development programme shall be reserved for LEADER. *(Extent: E+NI)*

6. At least 30 % of the total core[^V_NI_708eaefe-0959-44e5-2704-404d0911a0c8] contribution to the rural development programme shall be reserved for measures under the following Articles: Article 17 for environment and climate related investments; Articles 21, 28, 29 and 30, with the exception of Water Framework Directive related payments; and Articles 31, 32 and 34.

[^V_NI_415d4fce-d6ee-1b9d-25b7-5584375d172d]...

6a.[^V_NI_1d517d69-202f-9cc7-6ed5-47b341784119] The [^V_NI_7fc8e3a8-2b13-9e8a-5edf-d8f16e231e6c]... support provided under Article 39b shall not exceed 2 % of the total core[^V_NI_0708fcce-82a5-0e37-c16b-591c5c7e0e52] contribution to the rural development programme [^V_NI_81e05afc-fd57-ae2c-babb-01f182f9a091]....

7.[^V_NI_679f54aa-0241-f98e-fdcc-5acbf612de66] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

8.[^V_NI_1972a959-ad68-3105-a146-b0d909cd0824] An expenditure receiving support for rural development shall not be co-financed by way of a contribution from under Regulation 508/2014 or CMO support or direct payment support.

9. Public expenditure on aid to enterprises shall comply with the aid limits laid down in respect of State aid, unless this Regulation provides otherwise.

### Article 60. Eligibility of expenditure

1. By way of derogation from Article 65(9) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, in cases of emergency measures due to natural disasters, catastrophic events or adverse climatic events or a significant and sudden change in the socioeconomic conditions of the constituent nation[^c-01173ab0625b37b0e50c9b7838df2f48] or region, the rural development programmes may provide that eligibility of expenditure relating to programme changes may start from the date when the event occurred.

2. Expenditure shall be eligible for rural development support[^c-8c5edc119b457b95fbfacc1699b913c0] only where incurred for operations decided upon by the Managing Authority of the programme in question or under its responsibility, in accordance with selection criteria referred to in Article 49.

With the exception of general costs as defined in point (c) of Article 45(2), in respect of investment operations under measures in relation to the production of, and trade in, agricultural products[^c-1a676ef321afada2035730c4fd10f2d1], only expenditure which has been incurred after an application has been submitted to the competent authority shall be considered eligible. However, relevant authorities[^c-ddc2fbc3891ee138e21f8f0438d92868] may provide in their programme that expenditure which is related to emergency measures due to natural disasters, catastrophic events or adverse climatic events or a significant and sudden change in the socioeconomic conditions of the constituent nation[^c-3d46b60dabb61d79c1f95219698cf653] or region, and which has been incurred by the beneficiary after the event occurs, is also eligible.

Relevant authorities[^c-9281efef3ecde42d0a682b263de2a271] may provide in their programmes that only expenditure which has been incurred after the application for support has been approved by the competent authority shall be eligible.

3.[^c-bb989ba4483b63cc37d435a58641e5d4] Paragraphs 1 and 2 shall not apply to Article 51(1) and (2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bb989ba4483b63cc37d435a58641e5d4]

4. Payments by beneficiaries shall be supported by invoices and documents proving payment. Where this cannot be done, payments shall be supported by documents of equivalent probative value, except for the forms of support referred to in Article 67(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 other than under point (a) thereof.

### Article 61. Eligible expenditure

1. Where running costs are covered by support under this Regulation the following types of costs shall be eligible:

- (a) operating costs;
- (b) personnel costs;
- (c) training costs;
- (d) costs linked to public relations;
- (e) financial costs;
- (f) networking costs.

2. Studies shall only be eligible expenditure where they are linked to a specific operation under the programme or the specific objectives and targets of the programme.

3. Contributions in kind in the form of provision of works, goods, services, land and real estate for which no cash payment supported by invoices or documents of equivalent probative value has been made, may be eligible for support provided that the conditions of Article 69 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 are fulfilled.

### Article 62. Verifiability and controllability of measures[^c-ab6f062695aa6bdf7892a48a59f5eca6]

1. The relevant authority must ensure that all the rural development measures that they intend to implement are verifiable and controllable. The Managing Authority and paying agency must also undertake the assessment of the verifiability and controllability of measures during the implementation of the rural development programme. Assessment during the implementation period must take into account the results of controls in the previous and current programming period. Where the assessment reveals that the requirements of verifiability and controllability are not met, the measures concerned must be adjusted accordingly.

2. Where aid is granted on the basis of standard costs or additional costs and income foregone in accordance with points (a) and (b) of Article 21(1),as regards income foregone and maintenance costs, and with Articles 28 to 31, 33, and 34, the relevant authority[^c-df250929548de1be5c389eb57ba9a79b] shall ensure that the relevant calculations are adequate and accurate and established in advance on the basis of a fair, equitable and verifiable calculation method. To this end, a body that is functionally independent from the authorities responsible for the programme implementation and possesses the appropriate expertise shall perform the calculations or confirm the adequacy and accuracy of the calculations. A statement confirming the adequacy and accuracy of the calculations shall be included in the rural development programme.

### Article 63. Advances

1. Payment of advances shall *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0298d723f9354f42978334024a9c9771]may be subject to the establishment of a bank guarantee or an equivalent guarantee corresponding to 100 % of the amount of the advance. As regards public beneficiaries, advances shall be paid to municipalities, regional authorities and associations thereof, as well as to public law bodies.

A facility provided as a guarantee by a public authority shall be considered equivalent to the guarantee referred to in the first subparagraph, provided that the authority undertakes to pay the amount covered by that guarantee if entitlement to the advance paid is not established.

2. The guarantee may be released when the competent paying agency establishes that the amount of actual expenditure corresponding to the public contribution related to the operation exceeds the amount of the advance.

- (b (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] designated[^c-03e62afadac4ffba39be52a0e27f5018] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] paying agency within the meaning of Article 7 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (c (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3d0603b59f554b6f9eb6a6f18ae4e006][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The relevant authority[^c-09fb4938785ea895d88e070b4207f42d] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] shall ensure, for each rural development programme, that the relevant management and control system has been set up in such a way that ensures a clear allocation and separation of functions between the Managing Authority and other bodies. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The relevant authority *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] shall be responsible for ensuring that the systems function effectively throughout the programme period. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

4 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The relevant authority[^c-644bd795bb82bb98234f4d4009502114] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] shall clearly define the tasks of the Managing Authority, the Paying Agency and the local action groups under LEADER as regards to the application of eligibility and selection criteria and the project selection procedure. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

### Article 66 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Managing Authority[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The Managing Authority shall be responsible for managing and implementing the programme in an efficient, effective and correct way and in particular for: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

- (a (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] ensuring that there is an appropriate secure electronic system to record, maintain, manage and report statistical information on the programme and its implementation required for the purposes of monitoring and evaluation and, in particular, information required to monitor progress towards the defined objectives and priorities; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((b) (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c000006]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (c (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] ensuring that beneficiaries and other bodies involved in the implementation of operations: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
  - ((i)) are informed of their obligations resulting from the aid granted, and maintain either a separate accounting system or an adequate accounting code for all transactions relating to the operation; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
  - ((ii)) are aware of the requirements concerning the provision of data to the Managing Authority and the recording of outputs and results; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (d (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-119ef2427835010c2316ee5410308a09][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (e (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] ensuring that the evaluation plan referred to in Article 56 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is in place, that the ex post programme evaluation referred to in Article 57 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 is conducted within the time limits laid down in that Regulation, ensuring that such evaluations conform to the monitoring and evaluation system and submitting them to the Monitoring Committee *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-3e5d6226ca51ca9cf7db1c3e9accbd6e]... ; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (f (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] providing the Monitoring Committee with the information and documents needed to monitor implementation of the programme in the light of its specific objectives and priorities; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (g (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] drawing up the annual progress report, including aggregate monitoring tables *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] and submitting it to the Monitoring Committee for approval[^c-567457cbc1fa058af72d6c5df84a469c] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (h (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] ensuring that the paying agency receives all necessary information, in particular on the procedures operated and any controls carried out in relation to operations selected for funding, before payments are authorised; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- (i (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-b7e4ee0d0882c905d59d782a74d7c2fa][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-ec137574e2be599a170575c47f75dddf]... Managing Authority may designate one or more intermediate bodies including local authorities, regional development bodies or non-governmental organisations, to carry out the management and implementation of rural development operations. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

When a part of its tasks is delegated to another body, the Managing Authority shall retain full responsibility for the efficiency and correctness of management and implementation of those tasks. The Managing Authority shall ensure that appropriate provisions are in place to allow the other body to obtain all necessary data and information for execution of these tasks. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-971788e46cd7b223ab3fff5d7ba84af1][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

4 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-7c18fceb070dddb9d2ab4332d08b8b90][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] Subject to the role of the paying agencies and other bodies as set out in Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, a co-ordinating body may be designated with the purpose of ensuring consistency in the management of the programmes between constituent nations. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

5 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-c152e8721d0ce2761367c376003571bf][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

## TITLE VII — MONITORING AND EVALUATION

### CHAPTER I — General provisions

#### Section 1 — Establishment and objectives of a monitoring and evaluation system

##### Article 67 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Monitoring and evaluation system[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309][^c-802a324fa5f9e3899a1714b650590e90]

The appropriate authority may make regulations setting out a monitoring and evaluation system. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

##### Article 68 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Objectives[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

The monitoring and evaluation system shall aim to: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

- ((a)) demonstrate the progress and achievements of rural development policy and assess the impact, effectiveness, efficiency and relevance of rural development policy interventions; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((b)) contribute to better targeted support for rural development; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((c)) support a common learning process related to monitoring and evaluation. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

#### Section 2 — Technical provisions

##### Article 69 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Common indicators[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] A list of common indicators relating to the initial situation as well as to the financial execution, outputs, results and impact of the programme and applicable to each programme shall be specified in the monitoring and evaluation system provided for in Article 67 to allow for aggregation of data *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-fa48282a5cb7c1e7b1c5326f54bd220b].... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The common indicators shall be based on available data and linked to the structure and objectives of the rural development policy framework and shall allow assessment of the progress, efficiency and effectiveness of policy implementation against objectives and targets at *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-7c60031d15226d62d0e6b87809368448]... national and programme level. The common impact indicators shall be based on available data. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The evaluator shall quantify the impact of the programme measured by the impact indicators. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-eb1f0a2d71640a2a70b06eb50cf20e64]... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

##### Article 70 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Electronic Information System[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

Key information on the implementation of the programme, on each operation selected for funding, as well as on completed operations, needed for monitoring and evaluation, including key information on each beneficiary and project, shall be recorded and maintained electronically. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

##### Article 71 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Provision of information[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

Beneficiaries of support under rural development measures and local action groups shall undertake to provide to the Managing Authority and/or to appointed evaluators or other bodies delegated to perform functions on its behalf, all the information necessary to permit monitoring and evaluation of the programme, in particular in relation to meeting specified objectives and priorities. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

### CHAPTER II — Monitoring

#### Article 72 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Monitoring procedures[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The Managing Authority and the Monitoring Committee referred to in Article 47 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall monitor the quality of the implementation of the programme. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The Managing Authority and the Monitoring Committee shall carry out monitoring of each rural development programme by means of financial, output and target indicators. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

#### Article 73. Monitoring Committee[^c-5dd4696a172681dacb2dca58bce1bbbf]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 74 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Responsibilities of the Monitoring Committee[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

The Monitoring Committee shall satisfy itself as to the performance of the rural development programme and the effectiveness of its implementation. To that end, in addition to the functions referred to in Article 49 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 the Monitoring Committee shall: *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

- ((a)) be consulted and shall issue an opinion, before publication of the relevant call for proposals, on the selection criteria for financed operations, which shall be revised according to programming needs; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((b)) examine the activities and outputs related to the progress in the implementation of the evaluation plan of the programme; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((c)) examine, in particular, actions in the programme relating to the fulfilment of ex ante conditionalities, which fall within the responsibilities of the Managing Authority, and be informed of actions relating to the fulfilment of other ex ante conditionalities; *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((d)) participate in the national rural network to exchange information on programme implementation; and *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
- ((e)) consider and approve the annual implementation reports *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-4a5caf9956d3f4384305c42ce91184a2].... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

#### Article 75 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Annual implementation report[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

1 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^M_F_d750bdb1-2a1a-4d2b-cf7d-1b307d9daba7][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] By 30 June 2016 and by 30 June of each subsequent year until and including *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] 2026[^c-d0296e8e25530923854d01e186b6790b] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] 2024 *(Extent: E+N.I.)*[^c-87b1c715df92d72f840b05ba97b5865e] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309], the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] Managing Authority[^c-336398bf29de1217924f787e75c507eb] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] shall submit to the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] Monitoring Committee[^c-5a2310d720e6abe01e868efb4c9258cc] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] the annual implementation report on implementation of the rural development programme in the previous calendar year. The report submitted in 2016 shall cover the calendar years 2014 and 2015. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

2 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] In addition to complying with the requirements of Article 50 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 annual implementation reports shall include information inter alia on financial commitments and expenditure by measure, and a summary of the activities undertaken in relation to the evaluation plan. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-c66fd95b5bd1895f4212a5da40203c7c][^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

4 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] In addition to complying with the requirements of Article 50 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the annual implementation report submitted in 2019 shall also cover *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309][^c-eb89c389742e94a45eca8ef9dc7886d7]... an assessment of progress made in ensuring an integrated approach to use of *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-4f7b0861e2f11e253229e47dca605574]... financial instruments to support the territorial development of rural areas, including through local development strategies. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

5 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] appropriate authority may make regulations[^c-3e0e083e4f02f35b1138413e737daa9c] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309], laying down rules concerning the presentation of the annual implementation reports. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] [^c-b229e26d9088278a01bc16563be8c219]... *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

3 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] The evaluation reports shall be made available by the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] relevant authority[^M_F_355d3e77-d720-4eb5-cc9c-85bf3eb4e8e5] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309] on its website. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]
