## TITLE VIII — COMPETITION PROVISIONS

### Article 80 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Rules applying to undertakings[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

Where support under this Regulation is granted to forms of co-operation between undertakings, it may be granted only to such forms of co-operation which comply with the competition rules as they apply by virtue of Articles 206 to 210 of the Regulation of the European Parliament and the Council (EU) No 1308/2013. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bda7695099fa4ae5a9f91ec50e294309]

### Article 81. State aid[^c-6f8cb1857f869f56ddce4dd20cb1152f]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### Article 82. Additional financing[^c-5033205a45b5f2c30300a00d65b5af04]

Payments in relation to operations for the production of, and trade in, agricultural products, and intended to provide additional financing for rural development for which support is granted at any time during the programming period, shall be included by relevant authorities in the rural development programme as provided for in paragraph 1(j) of Article 8 *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-85e124bd1f1c35319beb9cdc1257f4fa] and, where they comply with the criteria under this Regulation, shall be approved by the relevant authority.

## TITLE IX — ... COMMON PROVISIONS AND TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS[^c-987ff6f6b656f9ebfc2173e403b4e5c6]

### CHAPTER I — Commission powers (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note)Powers (Extent: W)[^c-dd386912903fe467491383477153e09c]

#### Article 83. Regulations[^c-9780de301ecca21734b6c419ab2c0177]

1. Regulations made by the Secretary of State or the Welsh Ministers under this Regulation are to be made by statutory instrument.

2. For regulations made under this Regulation by the Scottish Ministers, see section 27 of the Interpretation and Legislative Reform (Scotland) Act 2010.

3. Any power of the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs to make regulations under this Regulation is exercisable by statutory rule for the purposes of the Statutory Rules (Northern Ireland) Order 1979.

4. Regulations under this Regulation may:

- (a) contain consequential, incidental, supplementary, transitional or saving provision (including provision amending, repealing or revoking enactments);
- (b) make different provision for different purposes.

5. Before making any regulations under this Regulation, the person or Department making the regulations must consult:

- (a) such bodies or persons as appear to the person or Department to be representative of the interests likely to be substantially affected by the regulations, and
- (b) such other bodies or persons as the person or Department may consider appropriate.

#### Article 84. Regulations: parliamentary procedure[^c-dda75d834f60c5fcf70152dee6a0763b]

1. A statutory instrument containing regulations made by the Secretary of State under this Regulation is subject to annulment in pursuance of a resolution of either House of Parliament.

2. A statutory instrument containing regulations made by the Welsh Ministers under this Regulation is subject to annulment in pursuance of a resolution of the National Assembly for Wales.

3. Regulations made by the Scottish Ministers under this Regulation are subject to the negative procedure (see section 28 of the Interpretation and Legislative Reform (Scotland) Act 2010).

4. Regulations made by the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs under this Regulation are subject to negative resolution within the meaning of section 41(6) of the Interpretation Act (Northern Ireland) 1954 as if they were a statutory instrument within the meaning of that Act.

### CHAPTER II — Common provisions

#### Article 85. Exchange of information and documents[^M_F_dde9e393-0a93-4d4e-fd5d-91c08f311ce9]

**No longer in force.**

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 86. Processing and protection of personal data

1. The relevant authority[^c-89f75188c0d05b4042bfe8b607dc7e60][^c-4feee7356dd696b8a811fbd334544b3c]... shall collect personal data for the purpose of carrying out their respective *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a9fd6c8f69f88a4d847090f059d11aa0] management control, monitoring and evaluation obligations under this Regulation, and in particular those laid down in Titles VI and VII, and shall not process this data in a way which is incompatible with this purpose.

2. Where personal data are processed for monitoring and evaluation purposes under Title VII using the secure electronic system referred to in Article 85, they shall be made anonymous, and processed in aggregated form only. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4e0bc84971ac16eb52072efdd7a5b070]

3. Personal data shall be processed in accordance with the rules of Directive 95/46/EC and Regulation (EC) No 45/2001 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f3805ba76df81d45eb12766b0536a0d4]Regulation (EU) No 2016/679 and the Data Protection Act 2018. In particular, such data shall not be stored in a form which permits identification of data subjects for longer than is necessary for the purposes for which they were collected or for which they are further processed, taking into account the minimum retention periods laid down in the law as it applies in the constituent nation[^c-6a9b1e2c2d75fa177e98113d884aad78].

4. The relevant authority shall inform the data subjects that their personal data may be processed by public[^c-6ac2b07e6bc779aab8c11ece9c853e82] bodies in accordance with paragraph 1 and that in this respect they enjoy the rights set out in the data protection rules of, respectively, Directive 95/46/EC and Regulation (EC) No 45/2001 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c505425afc8b70cffa1d1f617e994d80]Regulation (EU) No 2016/679 and the Data Protection Act 2018.

5. Articles 111 to 114 in Regulation(EU) 1306/2013 shall apply to this Article.

#### Article 87. General CAP provisions

Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and the provisions adopted pursuant to it shall apply in relation to the measures set out in this Regulation.

### CHAPTER III — Transitional and final provisions

#### Article 88 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 is repealed. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 shall continue to apply to operations implemented pursuant to programmes approved by the Commission under that Regulation before 1 January 2014. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

#### Article 89 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Transitional provisions[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

In order to facilitate the transition from the system established by Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 to the system established by this Regulation, the Commission shall be empowered to adopt delegated acts in accordance with Article 83, laying down the conditions under which support approved by the Commission under Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 may be integrated into support provided for under this Regulation, including for technical assistance and for the ex-post evaluations. Those delegated acts may also provide conditions for the transition from rural development support for Croatia under Regulation (EC) No 1085/2006 to support provided for under this Regulation. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

#### Article 90 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Entry into force and application[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

This Regulation shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0] *Official Journal of the European Union *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]*. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

It shall apply from 1 January 2014. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d7e26d48b964baf70accc020db1e6ff0]

## ANNEX III

**BIOPHYSICAL CRITERIA FOR THE DELIMITATION OF AREAS FACING NATURAL CONSTRAINTS**

<table>
<tfoot>
<tr><td colspan="3">Member States <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>The relevant authority <em>(Extent: W)</em> need only check fulfilment of this criterion against those of the thresholds that are relevant to the specific situation of an area</td></tr>
</tfoot>
<tbody>
<tr><th>CRITERION</th><th>DEFINITION</th><th>THRESHOLD</th></tr>
<tr><th colspan="3">CLIMATE</th></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="2">Low Temperature</td><td>Length of growing period (number of days) defined by number of days with daily average temperature &gt; 5 °C (LGPt5) or</td><td>≤ 180 days</td></tr>
<tr><td>Thermal-time sum (degree-days) for Growing Period defined by accumulated daily average temperature &gt; 5 °C</td><td>≤ 1 500 degree-days</td></tr>
<tr><td>Dryness</td><td>Ratio of the annual precipitation (P) to the annual potential evapotranspiration (PET)</td><td>P/PET ≤ 0.5</td></tr>
<tr><th colspan="3">CLIMATE AND SOIL</th></tr>
<tr><td>Excess Soil Moisture</td><td>Number of days at or above field capacity</td><td>≥ 230 days</td></tr>
<tr><th colspan="3">SOIL</th></tr>
<tr><td>Limited Soil Drainage</td><td>Areas which are water logged for significant duration of the year</td><td>Wet within 80 cm from the surface for over 6 months, or wet within 40 cm for over 11 months or<br>Poorly or very poorly drained soil or<br>Gleyic colour pattern within 40 cm from the surface</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="5">Unfavourable Texture and Stoniness</td><td rowspan="5">Relative abundance of clay, silt, sand, organic matter (weight %) and coarse material (volumetric %) fractions</td><td>≥ 15 % of topsoil volume is coarse material, including rock outcrop, boulder or</td></tr>
<tr><td>texture class in half or more (cumulatively) of the 100 cm soil surface is sand, loamy sand defined as:<br>Silt % + (2 × clay %) ≤ 30 % or</td></tr>
<tr><td>Topsoil texture class is heavy clay<br>(≥ 60 % clay) or</td></tr>
<tr><td>Organic soil (organic matter ≥ 30 %) of at least 40 cm or</td></tr>
<tr><td>Topsoil contains 30 % or more clay, and there are vertic properties within 100 cm of the soil surface</td></tr>
<tr><td>Shallow Rooting Depth</td><td>Depth (cm) from soil surface to coherent hard rock or hard pan</td><td>≤ 30 cm</td></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="3">Poor Chemical Properties</td><td rowspan="3">Presence of salts, exchangeable sodium, excessive acidity</td><td>Salinity: ≥ 4 deci-Siemens per meter (dS/m) in topsoil or</td></tr>
<tr><td>Sodicity: ≥ 6 Exchangeable Sodium Percentage (ESP) in half or more (cumulatively) of the 100 cm soil surface layer or</td></tr>
<tr><td>Soil Acidity: pH ≤ 5 (in water) in topsoil</td></tr>
<tr><th colspan="3">TERRAIN</th></tr>
<tr><td>Steep Slope</td><td>Change of elevation with respect to planimetric distance (%)</td><td>≥ 15 %</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<tr><td>RD priority 3: promoting food chain organisation, including processing and marketing of agricultural products, animal welfare and risk management in agriculture <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>TO 5: promoting climate change adaptation, risk prevention and management <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>3.1. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Risk prevention and risk management: the existence of national or regional risk assessments for disaster management. taking into account climate change adaptation <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>A national or regional risk assessment with the following elements shall be in place: <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>A description of the process, methodology, methods and non-sensitive data used for risk assessment as well as of the risk-based criteria for the prioritisation of investment; <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>A description of single-risk and multi-risk scenarios; <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Taking into account, where appropriate, national climate change adaptation strategies. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>

<tr><td>TO 5: promoting climate change adaptation, risk prevention and management <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>4.2 <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Minimum requirements for fertilisers and plant protection products: minimum requirements for fertilisers and plant protection products referred to in Article 28 of Chapter I of Title III of this Regulation are defined at national level <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>minimum requirements for fertilisers and plant protection products referred to in Chapter I of Title III of this Regulation are specified in the programmes; <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>

note)</em><br>Measures to ensure minimum requirements are in place related to the energy performance of buildings consistent with Articles 3, 4 and 5 of Directive 2010/31/EU of the European Parliament and the Council <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Measures necessary to establish a system of certification of the energy performance of buildings consistent with Article 11 of Directive 2010/31/EU <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Measures to ensure strategic planning on energy efficiency, consistent with Article 3 of Directive 2012/27 EU of the European Parliament and the Council <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Measures consistent with Article 13 of Directive 2006/32/EC of the European Parliament and the Council <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> on energy end-use efficiency and energy services to ensure the provision to final customers of individual meters in so far as it is technically possible, financially reasonable and proportionate in relation to the potential energy savings. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.2 <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Water sector: The existence of a) a water pricing policy which provides adequate incentives for users to use water resources efficiently and b) an adequate contribution of the different water uses to the recovery of the costs of water services at a rate determined in the approved river basin management plan for investment supported by the programmes. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>In sectors <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> funded by support for rural development <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>, a <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> relevant authority <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> has ensured a contribution of the different water uses to the recovery of the costs of water services by sector consistent with Article 9, paragraph 1 first indent of the Water Framework Directive having regard where appropriate, to the social, environmental and economic effects of the recovery as well as the geographic and climatic conditions of the region or regions affected. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.3 <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Renewable energy: Actions have been carried out to promote the production and distribution of renewable energy sources <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>Transparent support schemes, priority in grid access or guaranteed access and priority in dispatching, as well as standard rules relating to the bearing and sharing of costs of technical adaptations which have been made public are in place consistent with Article 14(1) and Article 16(2) and (3) of Directive 2009/28/EC <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>A <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> relevant authority <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> has adopted a national renewable energy action plan consistent with Article 4 of Directive 2009/28/EC <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
<tr><td>RD priority 6: promoting social inclusion poverty reduction and economic development in rural areas <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>TO 2: Enhancing access to, and use and quality of, information and communication technologies (Broadband target) <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>6. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>Next Generation Network (NGN) Infrastructure: The existence of national or regional NGA Plans which take account of regional actions in order to reach the <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ... high-speed Internet access targets, focusing on areas where the market fails to provide an open infrastructure at an affordable cost and of a quality in line with the <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ... competition and State aid rules and to provide accessible services to vulnerable groups. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td><td>A national or regional NGN Plan is in place that contains: <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>a plan of infrastructure investments based on an economic analysis taking account of existing private and public infrastructures and planned investments; <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>sustainable investment models that enhance competition and provide access to open, affordable, quality and future proof infrastructure and services; <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em><br>measures to stimulate private investment. <em>(Extent: W)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em></td></tr>
